### Suggestions & Next Steps

- Power the ESP32 from a clean 3.3 V rail: ≥100 µF bulk + 100 nF ceramic at the VDD pins, and a separate regulator for servos/motors — brownouts masquerade as random firmware bugs.
- Respect boot strapping pins (GPIO 0/2/12 on classic ESP32, GPIO 2/8/9 on C3): no external pulls that fight the straps, and never drive GPIO12 high at reset on 5 V-tolerant modules.
- Enable the hardware watchdog (esp_task_wdt) around your main loop and use deep-sleep with RTC retention for battery nodes.
- Gate every BPM/SpO₂ output behind a perfusion threshold (MIN_IR_LEVEL) + signal-quality index and a 5–6 s hold; never publish raw beats to UI.
- Calibrate the IR/RED ratio against a reference pulse oximeter at rest and motion to derive your personal SpO₂ curve; ambient-light cancellation (dark current subtraction) is mandatory in daylight.
- Capture 100 Hz raw accel/gyro logs across 20+ staged scenarios (bathroom slip, bed exit, trip, running) and set per-scenario thresholds; add orientation-change corroboration to cut false alarms before shipping.
- Regenerate this archive after every ChatGPT export (Settings → Data controls → Export) and diff against the previous INDEX.md to catch missing turns.
- Lock in the winning decision from this chat into your project docs/repo before the next ChatGPT session; context window loss is the main failure mode.

    { id: 'VT-005', name: 'Nikhil', age: 35, gender: 'Male', bed: 'Bed-05', ward: 'Critical Care 1', status: 'Stable', alert: 'No Alerts', photo: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1519085360753-af0119f7cbe7?q=80&w=150&auto=format&fit=crop', baselineHr: 75, baselineSpo2: 98, baselineTemp: 36.7, baselineResp: 15 },
    { id: 'VT-006', name: 'Pavan', age: 44, gender: 'Male', bed: 'Bed-06', ward: 'Critical Care 2', status: 'Good', alert: 'No Alerts', photo: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1506794778202-cad84cf45f1d?q=80&w=150&auto=format&fit=crop', baselineHr: 70, baselineSpo2: 98, baselineTemp: 36.5, baselineResp: 14 },
    { id: 'VT-007', name: 'Abdul', age: 59, gender: 'Male', bed: 'Bed-07', ward: 'Pulmonary unit', status: 'Watch', alert: 'High Heart Rate', photo: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1560250097-0b93528c311a?q=80&w=150&auto=format&fit=crop', baselineHr: 118, baselineSpo2: 96, baselineTemp: 37.1, baselineResp: 20 },
    { id: 'VT-008', name: 'Samuel', age: 38, gender: 'Male', bed: 'Bed-08', ward: 'Pulmonary unit', status: 'Critical', alert: 'Respiration Risk', photo: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1522075469751-3a6694fb2f61?q=80&w=150&auto=format&fit=crop', baselineHr: 91, baselineSpo2: 94, baselineTemp: 36.9, baselineResp: 26 },
    { id: 'VT-009', name: 'Taj', age: 47, gender: 'Male', bed: 'Bed-09', ward: 'Neonatology Sub', status: 'Stable', alert: 'No Alerts', photo: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1501196354995-cbb51c65aaea?q=80&w=150&auto=format&fit=crop', baselineHr: 78, baselineSpo2: 98, baselineTemp: 36.8, baselineResp: 16 },
    { id: 'VT-010', name: 'Krishna', age: 61, gender: 'Male', bed: 'Bed-10', ward: 'Neonatology Sub', status: 'Good', alert: 'No Alerts', photo: 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1492562080023-ab3db95bfbce?q=80&w=150&auto=format&fit=crop', baselineHr: 65, baselineSpo2: 99, baselineTemp: 36.4, baselineResp: 12 }
];

const getAvatarStyle = (id: string) => {
    const styles: Record<string, { bg: string; text: string; border: string }> = {
        'VT-001': { bg: 'bg-[#B76CF6]/10', text: 'text-[#B76CF6]', border: 'border-[#B76CF6]/25' },
        'VT-002': { bg: 'bg-blue-500/10', text: 'text-blue-500', border: 'border-blue-500/25' },
        'VT-003': { bg: 'bg-emerald-500/10', text: 'text-emerald-500', border: 'border-emerald-500/25' },
        'VT-004': { bg: 'bg-amber-500/10', text: 'text-amber-500', border: 'border-amber-500/25' },
        'VT-005': { bg: 'bg-rose-500/10', text: 'text-rose-500', border: 'border-rose-500/25' },
        'VT-006': { bg: 'bg-indigo-500/10', text: 'text-indigo-400', border: 'border-indigo-500/25' },
        'VT-007': { bg: 'bg-cyan-500/10', text: 'text-cyan-500', border: 'border-cyan-500/25' },
        'VT-008': { bg: 'bg-teal-500/10', text: 'text-teal-500', border: 'border-teal-500/25' },
        'VT-009': { bg: 'bg-violet-500/10', text: 'text-violet-400', border: 'border-violet-500/25' },
        'VT-010': { bg: 'bg-fuchsia-500/10', text: 'text-fuchsia-400', border: 'border-fuchsia-500/25' },
    };
    return styles[id] || { bg: 'bg-slate-500/10', text: 'text-slate-500', border: 'border-slate-500/25' };
};

interface BioDashboardProps {
    onBackToIntro: () => void;
    onNavigateToSlides: () => void;
}

export default function BioDashboard({ onBackToIntro, onNavigateToSlides }: BioDashboardProps) {
    const [activePatientId, setActivePatientId] = useState<string>(() => {
        try {
            const saved = localStorage.getItem('vi-trak-active-patient-id');
            if (saved && INITIAL_PATIENTS.some(p => p.id === saved)) {
                return saved;
            }
        } catch (e) {
            console.warn('Failed to read patient state from localStorage:', e);
        }
        return 'VT-001';
    });

    const [patientTelemetries, setPatientTelemetries] = useState<Record<string, Telemetry>>(() => {
        try {
            const saved = localStorage.getItem('vi-trak-patient-telemetries');
            if (saved) {
                return JSON.parse(saved);
            }
        } catch (e) {
            console.warn('Failed to parse patient-telemetries from localStorage:', e);
        }
        return INITIAL_PATIENTS.reduce((acc, p) => {
            acc[p.id] = {
                heartRate: p.baselineHr,
                spo2: p.baselineSpo2,
                temperature: p.baselineTemp,
                respiration: p.baselineResp
            };
            return acc;
        }, {} as Record<string, Telemetry>);
    });

    const [currentTime, setCurrentTime] = useState<string>('08:00:00');
    const [showExportModal, setShowExportModal] = useState<boolean>(false);
    const [exportSuccessMessage, setExportSuccessMessage] = useState<string>('');
    const [isDetailWindowOpen, setIsDetailWindowOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);

    // Interactive Clinical Diagnostic workspace states
    const [activeDiagTab, setActiveDiagTab] = useState<'live' | 'trends' | 'thresholds'>('live');
    const [hoveredTrendIndex, setHoveredTrendIndex] = useState<number | null>(null);

    // Custom user-adjustable safety triggers stored or defaults
    const [customThresholds, setCustomThresholds] = useState<Record<string, { minHr: number, maxHr: number, minSpo2: number, maxTemp: number }>>(() => {
        try {
            const saved = localStorage.getItem('vi-trak-custom-thresholds');
            if (saved) return JSON.parse(saved);
        } catch (e) { }
        return INITIAL_PATIENTS.reduce((acc, p) => {
            acc[p.id] = {
                minHr: 50,
                maxHr: 110,
                minSpo2: 93,
                maxTemp: 38.0
            };
            return acc;
        }, {} as Record<string, { minHr: number, maxHr: number, minSpo2: number, maxTemp: number }>);
    });

    // Hot clinical simulations matching active patient selections
    const [activeSimulatedIncident, setActiveSimulatedIncident] = useState<Record<string, 'None' | 'Tachycardia' | 'Hypoxia' | 'Fever'>>(() => {
        try {
            const saved = localStorage.getItem('vi-trak-simulated-incidents');
            if (saved) return JSON.parse(saved);
        } catch (e) { }
        return {};
    });

    // LocalStorage synchronizations
    useEffect(() => {
        try {
            localStorage.setItem('vi-trak-active-patient-id', activePatientId);
        } catch (e) {
            console.warn('Failed to write patient state to localStorage:', e);
        }
    }, [activePatientId]);

    useEffect(() => {
        try {
            localStorage.setItem('vi-trak-patient-telemetries', JSON.stringify(patientTelemetries));
        } catch (e) {
            console.warn('Failed to write patient telemetry to localStorage:', e);
        }
    }, [patientTelemetries]);

    useEffect(() => {
        try {
            localStorage.setItem('vi-trak-custom-thresholds', JSON.stringify(customThresholds));
        } catch (e) { }
    }, [customThresholds]);

    useEffect(() => {
        try {
            localStorage.setItem('vi-trak-simulated-incidents', JSON.stringify(activeSimulatedIncident));
        } catch (e) { }
    }, [activeSimulatedIncident]);

    // Clock updating loop
    useEffect(() => {
        const updateTime = () => {
            const now = new Date();
            setCurrentTime(now.toLocaleTimeString('en-US', { hour12: false }));
        };
        updateTime();
        const timer = setInterval(updateTime, 1000);
        return () => clearInterval(timer);
    }, []);

    // Multi-stream continuous physiology simulation with natural micro-fluctuations and simulated clinical incidents
    useEffect(() => {
        const interval = setInterval(() => {
            setPatientTelemetries(prev => {
                const next = { ...prev };
                INITIAL_PATIENTS.forEach(p => {
                    let hrBase = p.baselineHr;
                    let spo2Base = p.baselineSpo2;
                    let tempBase = p.baselineTemp;
                    let respBase = p.baselineResp;

                    // Check if there is an active simulated physical event trigger
                    const activeIncident = activeSimulatedIncident[p.id] || 'None';
                    if (activeIncident === 'Tachycardia') {
                        hrBase = 145;
                        respBase = 21;
                    } else if (activeIncident === 'Hypoxia') {
                        spo2Base = 85;
                        respBase = 29;
                        hrBase = Math.floor(hrBase * 1.2);
                    } else if (activeIncident === 'Fever') {
                        tempBase = 39.4;
                        hrBase = Math.floor(hrBase * 1.35);
                    } else {
                        // Fallback to initial preset alarm criteria mathematically inside streams
                        if (p.alert === 'High Fever') {
                            tempBase = 38.7;
                        } else if (p.alert === 'Low SpO₂') {
                            spo2Base = 91.5;
                            respBase = 22;
                        } else if (p.alert === 'High Heart Rate') {
                            hrBase = 120;
                        } else if (p.alert === 'Respiration Risk') {
                            respBase = 27;
                            spo2Base = 93.8;
                        } else if (p.alert === 'Critical Condition') {
                            hrBase = 114;
                            spo2Base = 89.2;
                            tempBase = 39.3;
                        }
                    }

                    // Natural biological fluctuations (high-fidelity noise representation)
                    const hrOffset = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 1.8;
                    const spo2Offset = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.35;
                    const tempOffset = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.08;
                    const respOffset = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.6;

                    next[p.id] = {
                        heartRate: Math.round(Math.max(40, Math.min(220, hrBase + hrOffset))),
                        spo2: Math.min(100, Math.max(70, Math.round(spo2Base + spo2Offset))),
                        temperature: parseFloat((tempBase + tempOffset).toFixed(2)),
                        respiration: Math.round(Math.max(6, Math.min(50, respBase + respOffset)))
                    };
                });
                return next;
            });
        }, 1500);

        return () => clearInterval(interval);
    }, [activeSimulatedIncident]);

    // 6 Days Historical Data generator formatted for CSV
    const getHistorical6DaysData = (ptId: string) => {
        const p = INITIAL_PATIENTS.find(item => item.id === ptId) || INITIAL_PATIENTS[0];
        const liveTel = patientTelemetries[p.id] || { heartRate: p.baselineHr, spo2: p.baselineSpo2, temperature: p.baselineTemp, respiration: p.baselineResp };

        // Variance multipliers keeping dataset organic across profiles
        const mult = p.id === 'VT-004' || p.id === 'VT-008' ? 1.15 : p.id === 'VT-003' || p.id === 'VT-007' ? 1.05 : 0.96;

        return [
            { date: '2026-05-15', day: 'Friday', heartRate: Math.round(p.baselineHr * 0.92 * mult), spo2: Math.min(100, p.baselineSpo2 + 1), temperature: parseFloat((p.baselineTemp - 0.1).toFixed(1)), respiration: Math.round(p.baselineResp * 0.9) },
            { date: '2026-05-16', day: 'Saturday', heartRate: Math.round(p.baselineHr * 1.04 * mult), spo2: Math.max(90, p.baselineSpo2 - 1), temperature: parseFloat((p.baselineTemp + 0.1).toFixed(1)), respiration: Math.round(p.baselineResp * 1.08) },
            { date: '2026-05-17', day: 'Sunday', heartRate: Math.round(p.baselineHr * 0.97 * mult), spo2: Math.min(100, p.baselineSpo2), temperature: parseFloat((p.baselineTemp - 0.2).toFixed(1)), respiration: Math.round(p.baselineResp * 0.95) },
            { date: '2026-05-18', day: 'Monday', heartRate: Math.round(p.baselineHr * 1.12 * mult), spo2: Math.max(90, p.baselineSpo2 - 2), temperature: parseFloat((p.baselineTemp + 0.2).toFixed(1)), respiration: Math.round(p.baselineResp * 1.2) },
            { date: '2026-05-19', day: 'Tuesday', heartRate: Math.round(p.baselineHr * 1.2 * mult), spo2: Math.min(100, p.baselineSpo2 + 1), temperature: parseFloat((p.baselineTemp + 0.3).toFixed(1)), respiration: Math.round(p.baselineResp * 1.25) },
            {
                date: '2026-05-20',
                day: 'Wednesday (Today)',
                heartRate: liveTel.heartRate,
                spo2: liveTel.spo2,
                temperature: liveTel.temperature,
                respiration: liveTel.respiration
            }
        ];
    };

    const generateCSVContent = (data: ReturnType<typeof getHistorical6DaysData>) => {
        const headers = 'Date,Day,Heart Rate (BPM),SpO2 (%),Temperature (°C),Respiration (RPM)\n';
        const rows = data.map(row =>
            `${row.date},${row.day},${row.heartRate},${row.spo2},${row.temperature.toFixed(1)},${row.respiration}`
        ).join('\n');
        return headers + rows;
    };

    const handleExportCSV = (ptId: string) => {
        const data = getHistorical6DaysData(ptId);
        const csvContent = generateCSVContent(data);
        const blob = new Blob([csvContent], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const link = document.createElement('a');
        link.setAttribute('href', url);
        const selectedPatient = INITIAL_PATIENTS.find(p => p.id === ptId) || INITIAL_PATIENTS[0];
        const sanitizedName = selectedPatient.name.toLowerCase();
        link.setAttribute('download', `vi_trak_6day_telemetry_${sanitizedName}_${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.csv`);
        document.body.appendChild(link);
        link.click();
        document.body.removeChild(link);

        setExportSuccessMessage(`CSV Exported successfully for client: ${selectedPatient.name}`);
        setTimeout(() => setExportSuccessMessage(''), 3000);
    };

    const handleCopyCSV = (ptId: string) => {
        const data = getHistorical6DaysData(ptId);
        const csvContent = generateCSVContent(data);
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(csvContent).then(() => {
            setExportSuccessMessage(`Copied telemetry records for ${INITIAL_PATIENTS.find(p => p.id === ptId)?.name} as CSV!`);
            setTimeout(() => setExportSuccessMessage(''), 3000);
        }).catch(err => {
            console.error('Text copy failed: ', err);
        });
    };

    // SVG Waveform generators for embedding medical visual flows per patient card
    const generateEcgWave = (width: number, height: number, hr: number) => {
        // Elegant physiological waveforms with standard PQR waves
        const normalizedHr = Math.max(50, Math.min(160, hr));
        const stepCount = 12;
        const paddingX = width / stepCount;
        let d = `M 0 ${height * 0.5} `;

        for (let i = 0; i < stepCount; i++) {
            const sx = i * paddingX;
            d += `L ${sx + paddingX * 0.3} ${height * 0.5} `;
            d += `Q ${sx + paddingX * 0.4} ${height * 0.43}, ${sx + paddingX * 0.45} ${height * 0.5} `; // P Wave
            d += `L ${sx + paddingX * 0.53} ${height * 0.5} `;
            d += `L ${sx + paddingX * 0.58} ${height * 0.55} `; // Q Dip
            d += `L ${sx + paddingX * 0.63} ${height * 0.15 + (160 - normalizedHr) * 0.08} `; // R tall peak
            d += `L ${sx + paddingX * 0.68} ${height * 0.75} `; // S Dip
            d += `L ${sx + paddingX * 0.72} ${height * 0.5} `;
            d += `Q ${sx + paddingX * 0.82} ${height * 0.38}, ${sx + paddingX * 0.88} ${height * 0.5} `; // T Wave
            d += `L ${sx + paddingX} ${height * 0.5} `;
        }
        return d;
    };

    const generateSimpleWave = (width: number, height: number, cycles: number, speed: number) => {
        let d = `M 0 ${height * 0.5} `;
        const pointsCount = 40;
        const step = width / pointsCount;
        for (let i = 0; i <= pointsCount; i++) {
            const x = i * step;
            const y = height * 0.5 + Math.sin((i * (cycles / pointsCount) * Math.PI * 2) - speed) * (height * 0.32);
            d += `L ${x} ${y} `;
        }
        return d;
    };

    return (
        <div className="relative w-full min-h-screen bg-[#F5F1F8] font-sans pb-16 flex flex-col text-[#15151B] selection:bg-[#D8B4FF]/40">

            {/* PREMIUM FLOATING BG BLUR COUPLING */}
            <div className="absolute top-0 inset-x-0 h-[500px] bg-gradient-to-b from-[#D8B4FF]/18 via-[#F5F1F8]/90 to-[#F5F1F8] -z-10 pointer-events-none" />
            <div className="absolute top-12 left-[12%] w-[400px] h-[400px] bg-white/25 rounded-full blur-[90px] -z-10 animate-float-slow" />
            <div className="absolute top-48 right-[10%] w-[450px] h-[450px] bg-[#B76CF6]/8 rounded-full blur-[100px] -z-10 animate-float-medium" />

            {/* TOP STATUS BAR - GORGEOUS GLASSMORPHISM */}
            <header className="w-full max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 pt-6">
                <div className="bg-white/55 backdrop-blur-2xl border border-white/80 rounded-[28px] p-4 sm:p-5 shadow-[0_16px_50px_rgba(183,108,246,0.04),0_1px_2px_rgba(255,255,255,0.7)] flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">

                    {/* Logo & Descriptor */}
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5">
                        <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-tr from-[#B76CF6] to-[#D8B4FF] flex items-center justify-center shadow-[0_8px_24px_rgba(183,108,246,0.25)] shrink-0 animate-heartbeat-slow">
                            <Heart className="w-5 h-5 text-white" />
                        </div>

                        <div>
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                <span className="font-display font-extrabold text-xl tracking-[0.15em] text-[#15151B] uppercase">
                                    VI TRAK <span className="text-[#B76CF6]">637</span>
                                </span>
                                <span className="text-[9px] uppercase font-mono tracking-widest bg-white/90 border border-slate-100 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-slate-500 shadow-sm leading-none font-bold">
                                    Clinical OS
                                </span>
                            </div>
                            <p className="text-[11px] text-[#73737C] font-semibold tracking-wide mt-0.5 uppercase">
                                Intelligent Bio-Monitoring System
                            </p>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Quick Real-Time Telemetry details & Actions in Status Bar */}
                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3 sm:gap-4">

                        {/* Live active beacon */}
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-[#35C56A]/10 border border-[#35C56A]/20 py-1.5 px-3 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-bold text-[#15151B] shadow-sm select-none">
                            <span className="relative flex h-2 w-2 shrink-0">
                                <span className="animate-ping absolute inline-flex h-full w-full rounded-full bg-[#35C56A] opacity-75" />
                                <span className="relative inline-flex rounded-full h-2 w-2 bg-[#35C56A]" />
                            </span>
                            <span className="font-mono tracking-wider">LIVE SYSTEM ACTIVE</span>
                        </div>

                        {/* Local Storage Host Replication Node */}
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-[#B76CF6]/10 border border-[#B76CF6]/25 py-1.5 px-3 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-mono text-[#B76CF6] font-bold uppercase select-none shadow-sm">
                            <Database className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-pulse" />
                            <span>LO_DB PERSISTED</span>
                        </div>

                        {/* Network Node */}
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200/50 py-1.5 px-3 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-mono text-slate-500 font-bold uppercase select-none">
                            <Wifi className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#B76CF6]" />
                            <span>Hosp-Net Sec_637</span>
                        </div>

                        {/* Notification Bubble */}
                        <div className="flex items-center justify-center w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-white/85 border border-slate-100 shadow-sm relative text-[#73737C]">
                            <Bell className="w-4.5 h-4.5" />
                            <span className="absolute top-1 right-1 w-2 h-2 bg-rose-500 rounded-full" />
                        </div>

                        {/* Live Clock */}
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-slate-100/75 py-1.5 px-3 rounded-xl font-mono text-[11px] font-black text-slate-700 tracking-wider">
                            <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
                            <span>{currentTime}</span>
                        </div>

                        {/* Back Button */}
                        <button
                            onClick={onBackToIntro}
                            className="p-1.5 rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-100 transition-colors cursor-pointer text-[#73737C] ml-1"
                            title="Return to System Initialization"
                        >
                            <Power className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-400 hover:text-rose-500" />
                        </button>
                    </div>

                </div>
            </header>

            {/* 10 IN-LINE INTERACTIVE CURRENT STATE CONTROLLER TAB BAR */}
            <section className="w-full max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 mt-6">
                <div className="bg-white/45 backdrop-blur-xl border border-white/70 p-3 sm:p-4 rounded-3xl shadow-[0_12px_36px_rgba(183,108,246,0.02)] flex flex-col gap-2.5">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-1.5 border-b border-purple-100/40 pb-2">
                        <span className="text-[10px] font-bold tracking-widest text-[#73737C] font-mono uppercase flex items-center gap-2">
                            <Activity className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#B76CF6] animate-pulse" />
                            Interactive Concurrent Clinician Hub • 10 Live Patient Stream Selectors
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-[9px] font-mono text-[#35C56A] tracking-wider font-extrabold bg-emerald-50 border border-[#35C56A]/20 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                            ALL CHANNELS SYNCHRONIZED
                        </span>
                    </div>

                    {/* 10 Inline Different Users Option List */}
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 overflow-x-auto pb-1.5 pr-1 scrollbar-thin">
                        {INITIAL_PATIENTS.map((pt, idx) => {
                            const tel = patientTelemetries[pt.id] || { heartRate: pt.baselineHr, spo2: pt.baselineSpo2, temperature: pt.baselineTemp, respiration: pt.baselineResp };
                            const isSelected = pt.id === activePatientId;

                            const isCriticalState = tel.spo2 < 93 || tel.temperature >= 38.5;
                            const isWatchState = tel.heartRate > 105 || tel.temperature > 37.3;

                            const statusDot = isCriticalState
                                ? 'bg-rose-500 shadow-[0_0_6px_rgba(255,77,109,0.5)]'
                                : isWatchState
                                    ? 'bg-[#FF9D2E] shadow-[0_0_6px_rgba(255,157,46,0.5)]'
                                    : 'bg-[#35C56A] shadow-[0_0_6px_rgba(53,197,106,0.5)]';

                            return (
                                <button
                                    key={pt.id}
                                    onClick={() => {
                                        setActivePatientId(pt.id);
                                        setIsDetailWindowOpen(true);
                                    }}
                                    className={`flex items-center gap-3 py-2 px-4 rounded-2xl border text-xs font-bold transition-all shrink-0 cursor-pointer select-none ${isSelected
                                        ? 'bg-white border-[#B76CF6] text-[#B76CF6] shadow-[0_8px_20px_rgba(183,108,246,0.1)]'
                                        : 'bg-white/40 border-white/80 text-slate-600 hover:bg-white/70 hover:border-slate-300'
                                        }`}
                                >
                                    <span className={`w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full ${statusDot}`} />
                                    <span className="font-semibold text-[10px] text-slate-400 font-mono uppercase">CH-{idx + 1}</span>
                                    <span className="tracking-tight text-[13px]">{pt.name}</span>
                                    <span className="font-mono text-[10px] bg-slate-500/10 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded font-black text-slate-700">
                                        {tel.heartRate} <span className="text-[8px] font-normal">BPM</span>
                                    </span>
                                </button>
                            );
                        })}
                    </div>
                </div>
            </section>

            {/* EXPORT DATA POPUP SYSTEM NOTIFICATION BOX */}
            <AnimatePresence>
                {exportSuccessMessage && (
                    <div className="w-full max-w-7xl mx-auto px-4 sm:px-6 mt-4">
                        <motion.div
                            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: -10 }}
                            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
                            exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -10 }}
                            className="bg-emerald-500 text-white font-semibold text-xs py-3 px-5 rounded-2xl shadow-md flex items-center justify-between gap-4"
                        >
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                <Check className="w-4.5 h-4.5 text-white stroke-[3px]" />
                                <span>{exportSuccessMessage}</span>
                            </div>
                            <button
                                onClick={() => setExportSuccessMessage('')}
                                className="opacity-70 hover:opacity-100 cursor-pointer text-white"
                            >
                                <X className="w-4 h-4" />
                            </button>
                        </motion.div>
                    </div>
                )}
            </AnimatePresence>

            {/* EXPORTING BIO-METRICS MODAL DIALOG */}
            {showExportModal && (
                <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-[#15151B]/40 backdrop-blur-md z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
                    <motion.div
                        initial={{ scale: 0.96, opacity: 0 }}
                        animate={{ scale: 1, opacity: 1 }}
                        exit={{ scale: 0.96, opacity: 0 }}
                        className="bg-white/95 backdrop-blur-3xl border border-white/90 rounded-[36px] p-6 sm:p-8 max-w-xl w-full shadow-[0_32px_80px_rgba(183,108,246,0.18)] text-[#15151B]"
                    >
                        {/* Modal Header */}
                        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4 border-b border-purple-50 pb-5">
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-[#B76CF6]/10 flex items-center justify-center text-[#B76CF6]">
                                    <FileText className="w-5 h-5" />
                                </div>
                                <div>
                                    <h3 className="text-lg font-extrabold tracking-tight text-slate-800">Biometric Export Console</h3>
                                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-medium mt-0.5">
                                        Stream format calibrated for Clinical EHR Database registries.
                                    </p>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                            <button
                                onClick={() => setShowExportModal(false)}
                                className="p-1 px-1.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-400 font-bold transition-colors cursor-pointer"
                            >
                                <X className="w-5 h-5" />
                            </button>
                        </div>

                        {/* Modal Content - Patient Details */}
                        <div className="mt-5 space-y-4">
                            <div className="bg-slate-50/70 border border-slate-100 p-4.5 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-between">
                                <div>
                                    <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest leading-none font-bold">ACTIVE PATIENT MATCH</span>
                                    <h4 className="text-base font-extrabold text-[#15151B] mt-0.5">
                                        {INITIAL_PATIENTS.find(p => p.id === activePatientId)?.name}
                                    </h4>
                                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 font-semibold mt-0.5">
                                        ID: {activePatientId} • {INITIAL_PATIENTS.find(p => p.id === activePatientId)?.ward} • {INITIAL_PATIENTS.find(p => p.id === activePatientId)?.bed}
                                    </p>
                                </div>
                                <div className="text-right">
                                    <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest font-bold">HISTORY SCOPE</span>
                                    <p className="text-xs text-[#B76CF6] font-extrabold mt-0.5 uppercase tracking-wide">6 Continuous Days</p>
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            {/* Grid Preview */}
                            <div className="border border-slate-100 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden bg-slate-50/50">
                                <div className="bg-slate-50 p-2.5 font-mono text-[9px] uppercase tracking-widest text-[#73737C] font-bold border-b border-slate-100">
                                    EXPORT TELEMETRY DATA PREVIEW
                                </div>
                                <div className="overflow-x-auto max-h-[160px] scrollbar-thin">
                                    <table className="w-full text-left text-[11px] font-medium border-collapse">
                                        <thead>
                                            <tr className="border-b border-indigo-50/50 text-[9px] uppercase font-mono text-slate-400">
                                                <th className="p-2 pl-3">Date</th>
                                                <th className="p-2">HR (BPM)</th>
                                                <th className="p-2">SpO₂ (%)</th>
                                                <th className="p-2">Temp (°C)</th>
                                                <th className="p-2 pr-3">Resp (RPM)</th>
                                            </tr>
                                        </thead>
                                        <tbody>
                                            {getHistorical6DaysData(activePatientId).map((row, index) => (
                                                <tr key={row.date} className={`border-b border-slate-100/40 ${index === 5 ? 'bg-purple-500/5 text-[#B76CF6] font-bold' : 'text-slate-600'}`}>
                                                    <td className="p-2 pl-3 font-mono">{row.date}</td>
                                                    <td className="p-2 font-mono">{row.heartRate}</td>
                                                    <td className="p-2 font-mono">{row.spo2}%</td>
                                                    <td className="p-2 font-mono">{row.temperature.toFixed(1)}°C</td>
                                                    <td className="p-2 pr-4 font-mono">{row.respiration}</td>
                                                </tr>
                                            ))}
                                        </tbody>
                                    </table>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        {/* Modal Actions */}
                        <div className="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
                            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-widest font-bold">
                                FORMAT: Standard RFC4180 CSV
                            </span>
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                <button
                                    onClick={() => {
                                        handleCopyCSV(activePatientId);
                                        setShowExportModal(false);
                                    }}
                                    className="bg-white border border-slate-200 hover:border-slate-300 py-2 px-4 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-slate-600 transition-all shadow-sm cursor-pointer"
                                >
                                    Copy CSV
                                </button>
                                <button
                                    onClick={() => {
                                        handleExportCSV(activePatientId);
                                        setShowExportModal(false);
                                    }}
                                    className="bg-[#B76CF6] hover:bg-[#a15ada] py-2 px-4.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-white transition-all shadow-md hover:shadow-lg cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1.5"
                                >
                                    <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                                    <span>Download file</span>
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </motion.div>
                </div>
            )}

            {/* Master Interactive Diagnostics clinical workspace floating modal window */}
            <AnimatePresence>
                {isDetailWindowOpen && (
                    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-[#15151B]/60 backdrop-blur-md overflow-y-auto flex items-center justify-center p-4 md:p-6 select-none animate-fade-in">
                        <motion.div
                            initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95, y: 30 }}
                            animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1, y: 0 }}
                            exit={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95, y: 30 }}
                            transition={{ duration: 0.4, ease: [0.16, 1, 0.3, 1] }}
                            className="relative w-full max-w-6xl bg-white/95 backdrop-blur-3xl border border-white p-5 sm:p-7 rounded-[40px] shadow-[0_32px_80px_rgba(183,108,246,0.22)] my-8 transition-all"
                        >

                            {/* Floating Close Button */}
                            <button
                                onClick={() => setIsDetailWindowOpen(false)}
                                className="absolute top-5 right-5 w-10 h-10 rounded-full bg-slate-100 hover:bg-rose-500 hover:text-white flex items-center justify-center text-slate-500 transition-all cursor-pointer shadow-sm border border-slate-200/50 z-20"
                                title="Return to Channel Grid"
                            >
                                <X className="w-5 h-5" />
                            </button>

                            {(() => {
                                const selectedPatient = INITIAL_PATIENTS.find(p => p.id === activePatientId) || INITIAL_PATIENTS[0];
                                const tel = patientTelemetries[selectedPatient.id] || { heartRate: selectedPatient.baselineHr, spo2: selectedPatient.baselineSpo2, temperature: selectedPatient.baselineTemp, respiration: selectedPatient.baselineResp };

                                // Evaluate safety thresholds
                                const thresholds = customThresholds[selectedPatient.id] || { minHr: 50, maxHr: 110, minSpo2: 93, maxTemp: 38.0 };
                                const isHrOver = tel.heartRate > thresholds.maxHr;
                                const isHrUnder = tel.heartRate < thresholds.minHr;
                                const isSpo2Low = tel.spo2 < thresholds.minSpo2;
                                const isTempHigh = tel.temperature > thresholds.maxTemp;
                                const isAnyThresholdBreached = isHrOver || isHrUnder || isSpo2Low || isTempHigh;

                                const avStyle = getAvatarStyle(selectedPatient.id);
                                const activeIncident = activeSimulatedIncident[selectedPatient.id] || 'None';

                                const historicalDataForActive = getHistorical6DaysData(selectedPatient.id);
                                const activeHoveredTrend = hoveredTrendIndex !== null ? historicalDataForActive[hoveredTrendIndex] : null;

                                return (
                                    <div className="bg-transparent p-0 border-0 rounded-none shadow-none mt-2 transition-all">

                                        {/* Selected Patient Identity Banner */}
                                        <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row items-stretch lg:items-center justify-between gap-5 border-b border-purple-100/40 pb-5">
                                            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4">
                                                {/* Avatar wrapper */}
                                                <div className={`w-14 h-14 rounded-full border-2 ${avStyle.border} ${avStyle.bg} flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-sm relative`}>
                                                    <User className={`w-6.5 h-6.5 ${avStyle.text}`} />
                                                    <span className={`absolute bottom-0 right-0 w-3.5 h-3.5 rounded-full border-2 border-white ${selectedPatient.status === 'Critical' || isAnyThresholdBreached ? 'bg-rose-500 animate-pulse' :
                                                        selectedPatient.status === 'Watch' ? 'bg-[#FF9D2E]' : 'bg-[#35C56A]'
                                                        }`} />
                                                </div>

                                                <div>
                                                    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                                                        <h2 className="text-xl font-extrabold text-[#15151B] tracking-tight">{selectedPatient.name}</h2>
                                                        <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-mono tracking-wider bg-purple-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[#B76CF6] font-bold border border-purple-100">
                                                            {selectedPatient.id}
                                                        </span>
                                                        {activeIncident !== 'None' && (
                                                            <span className="text-[9px] font-mono tracking-wider bg-rose-500 text-white font-black px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full uppercase animate-pulse">
                                                                Simulating: {activeIncident}
                                                            </span>
                                                        )}
                                                    </div>
                                                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-1 font-semibold">
                                                        Age: {selectedPatient.age} • Gender: {selectedPatient.gender} • Location: <span className="text-[#B76CF6]">{selectedPatient.ward} / {selectedPatient.bed}</span>
                                                    </p>
                                                </div>
                                            </div>

                                            {/* Switcher Navigation Tabs */}
                                            <div className="flex items-center self-start lg:self-auto overflow-x-auto bg-slate-100/80 p-1.5 rounded-2xl select-none gap-1">
                                                <button
                                                    onClick={() => setActiveDiagTab('live')}
                                                    className={`py-2 px-4.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap ${activeDiagTab === 'live'
                                                        ? 'bg-white text-[#B76CF6] shadow-sm'
                                                        : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-900'
                                                        }`}
                                                >
                                                    Live Oscilloscopes
                                                </button>

                                                <button
                                                    onClick={() => setActiveDiagTab('trends')}
                                                    className={`py-2 px-4.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap ${activeDiagTab === 'trends'
                                                        ? 'bg-white text-[#B76CF6] shadow-sm'
                                                        : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-900'
                                                        }`}
                                                >
                                                    6-Day Clinical Trends
                                                </button>

                                                <button
                                                    onClick={() => setActiveDiagTab('thresholds')}
                                                    className={`py-2 px-4.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all cursor-pointer whitespace-nowrap ${activeDiagTab === 'thresholds'
                                                        ? 'bg-white text-[#B76CF6] shadow-sm'
                                                        : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-900'
                                                        }`}
                                                >
                                                    Safety Thresholds & Simulator
                                                </button>
                                            </div>
                                        </div>

                                        {/* Threshold Alarm Flash Event */}
                                        {isAnyThresholdBreached && (
                                            <div className="mt-4 bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/30 p-3.5 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-between animate-pulse">
                                                <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 text-rose-600">
                                                    <ShieldAlert className="w-5 h-5 shrink-0" />
                                                    <span className="text-xs font-extrabold tracking-wider uppercase font-mono">
                                                        CRITICAL BREACH TRIGGERED: {isHrOver ? 'HIGH BPM BURST' : isHrUnder ? 'BRADYPNEA WARNING' : isSpo2Low ? 'SEVERE ARTERIAL DESATURATION' : isTempHigh ? 'PYREXIA FEVER' : 'THRESHOLD ALARM'}
                                                    </span>
                                                </div>
                                                <span className="text-[9px] font-mono font-black tracking-widest bg-rose-500 text-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                                                    CLINICAL PATIENT RISK STATE
                                                </span>
                                            </div>
                                        )}

                                        {/* WORKSPACE AREA */}
                                        <div className="mt-6">

                                            {/* TAB 1: Real-time Oscilloscopes (Graphical & Numerical Displays) */}
                                            {activeDiagTab === 'live' && (
                                                <div className="space-y-5">
                                                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between pb-1">
                                                        <span className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase text-slate-400 font-extrabold tracking-widest">
                                                            MULTI-PARAMETER WAVEFORM STREAMS • LIVE INTERACTIVE HUD
                                                        </span>
                                                        <span className="text-[9.5px] font-mono text-[#35C56A] font-bold bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-100 px-2 rounded">
                                                            ● REAL-TIME DISPATCHER COMPLIANT
                                                        </span>
                                                    </div>

                                                    {/* Dark Hospital Scope Monitor Board */}
                                                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 xl:grid-cols-12 gap-5">

                                                        {/* Interactive scope waveforms block */}
                                                        <div className="xl:col-span-8 bg-[#111116] rounded-3xl p-5 border border-slate-900 shadow-inner relative overflow-hidden group">

                                                            {/* Thin vector medical grid lines overlay */}
                                                            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-[linear-gradient(rgba(183,108,246,0.03)_1px,transparent_1px),linear-gradient(90deg,rgba(183,108,246,0.03)_1px,transparent_1px)] bg-[size:16px_16px] pointer-events-none" />

                                                            {/* Hovering sweeping crosshair HUD */}
                                                            <div className="absolute top-2 right-4 text-[9px] font-mono text-indigo-400 font-bold select-none uppercase tracking-widest opacity-60">
                                                                Sweep Speed: 25 mm/s • Scale: 10 mm/mV
                                                            </div>

                                                            <div className="space-y-4">
                                                                {/* Heart Rate / Pulse ECG Waveform panel */}
                                                                <div>
                                                                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-mono text-rose-400 font-bold mb-1">
                                                                        <span>PARAMETER I: LEAD II (ECG / HR)</span>
                                                                        <span className="bg-rose-500/10 px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded font-black">{tel.heartRate} BPM (Live)</span>
                                                                    </div>
                                                                    <div className="h-16 bg-black/40 rounded-xl relative overflow-hidden border border-slate-900/60">
                                                                        <svg className="w-full h-full text-rose-500 stroke-current drop-shadow-[0_0_4px_rgba(239,68,68,0.6)]" viewBox="0 0 400 35" preserveAspectRatio="none">
                                                                            <motion.path
                                                                                d={generateEcgWave(400, 35, tel.heartRate)}
                                                                                fill="none"
                                                                                strokeWidth="1.8"
                                                                                animate={{ x: [-20, 0] }}
                                                                                transition={{ repeat: Infinity, duration: tel.heartRate > 110 ? 0.8 : 1.5, ease: "linear" }}
                                                                            />
                                                                        </svg>
                                                                    </div>
                                                                </div>

                                                                {/* SpO2 Saturation Waveform panel */}
                                                                <div>
                                                                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-mono text-emerald-400 font-bold mb-1">
                                                                        <span>PARAMETER II: PLETHYSMOGRAPH (SpO₂ / OXYGENATION)</span>
                                                                        <span className="bg-emerald-500/10 px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded font-black">{tel.spo2}% SpO₂</span>
                                                                    </div>
                                                                    <div className="h-14 bg-black/40 rounded-xl relative overflow-hidden border border-slate-900/60">
                                                                        <svg className="w-full h-full text-[#35C56A] stroke-current drop-shadow-[0_0_4px_rgba(53,197,106,0.6)]" viewBox="0 0 400 30" preserveAspectRatio="none">
                                                                            <motion.path
                                                                                d={generateSimpleWave(400, 30, 15, 3)}
                                                                                fill="none"
                                                                                strokeWidth="1.8"
                                                                                animate={{ x: [-25, 0] }}
                                                                                transition={{ repeat: Infinity, duration: 1.6, ease: "linear" }}
                                                                            />
                                                                        </svg>
                                                                    </div>
                                                                </div>

                                                                {/* Respirations Waveform panel */}
                                                                <div>
                                                                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-mono text-blue-400 font-bold mb-1">
                                                                        <span>PARAMETER III: CAPNOGRAPHY CO₂ (RR / PULMONARY VENTILATION)</span>
                                                                        <span className="bg-blue-500/10 px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded font-black">{tel.respiration} RPM</span>
                                                                    </div>
                                                                    <div className="h-14 bg-black/40 rounded-xl relative overflow-hidden border border-slate-900/60">
                                                                        <svg className="w-full h-full text-blue-400 stroke-current drop-shadow-[0_0_4px_rgba(96,165,250,0.6)]" viewBox="0 0 400 30" preserveAspectRatio="none">
                                                                            <motion.path
                                                                                d={generateSimpleWave(400, 30, 8, 1)}
                                                                                fill="none"
                                                                                strokeWidth="1.8"
                                                                                animate={{ x: [-15, 0] }}
                                                                                transition={{ repeat: Infinity, duration: 2.8, ease: "linear" }}
                                                                            />
                                                                        </svg>
                                                                    </div>
                                                                </div>

                                                                {/* Vascular Pulse Rate Tracker */}
                                                                <div>
                                                                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] font-mono text-purple-400 font-bold mb-1">
                                                                        <span>PARAMETER IV: VASCULAR RESPONSE PROFILE (PR / PULSE TRACKER)</span>
                                                                        <span className="bg-purple-500/10 px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded font-black">{tel.heartRate} BPM (Vascular)</span>
                                                                    </div>
                                                                    <div className="h-14 bg-black/40 rounded-xl relative overflow-hidden border border-slate-900/60">
                                                                        <svg className="w-full h-full text-purple-400 stroke-current drop-shadow-[0_0_4px_rgba(183,108,246,0.6)]" viewBox="0 0 400 30" preserveAspectRatio="none">
                                                                            <motion.path
                                                                                d={generateEcgWave(400, 30, tel.heartRate * 0.98)}
                                                                                fill="none"
                                                                                strokeWidth="1.6"
                                                                                animate={{ x: [-20, 0] }}
                                                                                transition={{ repeat: Infinity, duration: tel.heartRate > 110 ? 0.82 : 1.52, ease: "linear" }}
                                                                            />
                                                                        </svg>
                                                                    </div>
                                                                </div>

                                                            </div>
                                                        </div>

                                                        {/* Side Numerical Diagnostic Readouts and Averages */}
                                                        <div className="xl:col-span-4 flex flex-col justify-between gap-4.5">
                                                            <div className="bg-slate-50/80 border border-slate-100 rounded-2xl p-4.5">
                                                                <span className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase text-slate-400 font-bold tracking-widest leading-none">CLINICAL OBSERVATION HUD</span>

                                                                <div className="mt-4.5 space-y-4">
                                                                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                                                                        <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold uppercase flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                                                            <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-rose-500 inline-block animate-ping" />
                                                                            Cardiac Pulse (HR)
                                                                        </span>
                                                                        <span className="font-mono text-sm font-extrabold text-[#15151B]">{tel.heartRate} BPM</span>
                                                                    </div>

                                                                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                                                                        <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold uppercase flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                                                            <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 inline-block" />
                                                                            Oxygen Sat (SpO₂)
                                                                        </span>
                                                                        <span className="font-mono text-sm font-extrabold text-[#15151B]">{tel.spo2}%</span>
                                                                    </div>

                                                                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                                                                        <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold uppercase flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                                                            <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-blue-500 inline-block" />
                                                                            Respirations (RR)
                                                                        </span>
                                                                        <span className="font-mono text-sm font-extrabold text-[#15151B]">{tel.respiration} /min</span>
                                                                    </div>

                                                                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                                                                        <span className="text-xs text-slate-500 font-semibold uppercase flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                                                            <span className="w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-amber-500 inline-block" />
                                                                            Temp (Core Body)
                                                                        </span>
                                                                        <span className="font-mono text-sm font-extrabold text-[#15151B]">{tel.temperature.toFixed(2)} °C</span>
                                                                    </div>
                                                                </div>
                                                            </div>

                                                            {/* Summary diagnostic checklist */}
                                                            <div className="bg-purple-500/5 border border-[#B76CF6]/15 rounded-2xl p-4.5 flex-1">
                                                                <span className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase text-[#B76CF6] font-bold tracking-widest">
                                                                    DIAGNOSTIC STATUS LOG
                                                                </span>

                                                                <div className="mt-3.5 space-y-2.5 text-xs text-slate-600 font-medium">
                                                                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                                                        {tel.spo2 >= thresholds.minSpo2 ? (
                                                                            <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-[#35C56A] shrink-0" />
                                                                        ) : (
                                                                            <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-500 shrink-0" />
                                                                        )}
                                                                        <span>Arterial Saturations: {tel.spo2 >= thresholds.minSpo2 ? 'HYPOXIA THREAT CLEAR' : 'DESATURATION ALARM BREACHED'}</span>
                                                                    </div>

                                                                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                                                        {tel.heartRate <= thresholds.maxHr && tel.heartRate >= thresholds.minHr ? (
                                                                            <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-[#35C56A] shrink-0" />
                                                                        ) : (
                                                                            <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-500 shrink-0 animate-pulse" />
                                                                        )}
                                                                        <span>Heart Rate: {tel.heartRate > thresholds.maxHr ? 'TACHYCARDIA BURST' : tel.heartRate < thresholds.minHr ? 'BRADYPNEA WARNING' : 'NORMAL SINUS PROFILE'}</span>
                                                                    </div>

                                                                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                                                                        {tel.temperature <= thresholds.maxTemp ? (
                                                                            <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-[#35C56A] shrink-0" />
                                                                        ) : (
                                                                            <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-rose-500 shrink-0" />
                                                                        )}
                                                                        <span>Core Temp Drift: {tel.temperature > thresholds.maxTemp ? 'HYPERTHERMIA SPIKE' : 'NOMINAL STABLE FEVER FREE'}</span>
                                                                    </div>
                                                                </div>
                                                            </div>

                                                        </div>

                                                    </div>
                                                </div>
                                            )}

                                            {/* TAB 2: Historical 6-Day Trends Graph (Analytical Panel) */}
                                            {activeDiagTab === 'trends' && (
                                                <div className="space-y-5">
                                                    <div className="bg-[#B76CF6]/5 border border-[#B76CF6]/10 p-4.5 rounded-2xl flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
                                                        <div>
                                                            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono text-[#B76CF6] uppercase font-bold tracking-widest leading-none">ANALYTICS COMPILER</span>
                                                            <h4 className="text-sm font-extrabold text-[#15151B] mt-1">Multi-Day Physiological Trends Timeline</h4>
                                                            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                                                                Hover across the days of the week below to inspect numeric averages compiled for {selectedPatient.name}.
                                                            </p>
                                                        </div>

                                                        {/* Display hovered metrics bubble */}
                                                        <div className="bg-white px-4 py-2 border border-purple-50 rounded-xl shadow-sm text-xs font-bold font-mono tracking-tight shrink-0">
                                                            {activeHoveredTrend ? (
                                                                <div className="text-left">
                                                                    <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-[#B76CF6] block">{activeHoveredTrend.day}</span>
                                                                    <span className="text-slate-700">HR: {activeHoveredTrend.heartRate} • SpO₂: {activeHoveredTrend.spo2}% • Temp: {activeHoveredTrend.temperature}°C • RR: {activeHoveredTrend.respiration}</span>
                                                                </div>
                                                            ) : (
                                                                <span className="text-slate-400 italic">Hover any bar columns below to inspect records</span>
                                                            )}
                                                        </div>
                                                    </div>

                                                    {/* Historical Trends Visual Grid Bar graph */}
                                                    <div className="border border-purple-50 bg-slate-50/50 p-6 rounded-3xl">
                                                        <div className="flex items-end justify-between gap-4 h-[200px] pb-4 border-b border-purple-100/40 relative">

                                                            {/* Horizontal benchmark indicators */}
                                                            <div className="absolute left-0 right-0 top-1/4 border-t border-purple-100/10 pointer-events-none" />
                                                            <div className="absolute left-0 right-0 top-2/4 border-t border-purple-100/10 pointer-events-none" />
                                                            <div className="absolute left-0 right-0 top-3/4 border-t border-purple-100/10 pointer-events-none" />

                                                            {historicalDataForActive.map((dayRow, barIdx) => {
                                                                const isHovered = barIdx === hoveredTrendIndex;
                                                                // calculate visual heights based on Heart Rate as high anchor
                                                                const barHeightPercent = Math.min(100, Math.max(25, (dayRow.heartRate / 150) * 100));

                                                                return (
                                                                    <div
                                                                        key={dayRow.date}
                                                                        onMouseEnter={() => setHoveredTrendIndex(barIdx)}
                                                                        onMouseLeave={() => setHoveredTrendIndex(null)}
                                                                        className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center group/trend cursor-pointer"
                                                                    >
                                                                        {/* Glowing metrics indicator bubble inside column bar */}
                                                                        <div className="w-full relative flex flex-col justify-end h-[160px]">
                                                                            <motion.div
                                                                                className={`w-full rounded-t-xl transition-all ${isHovered
                                                                                    ? 'bg-gradient-to-t from-[#B76CF6] to-[#D8B4FF] shadow-[0_4px_16px_rgba(183,108,246,0.3)]'
                                                                                    : 'bg-indigo-200 hover:bg-indigo-300'
                                                                                    }`}
                                                                                style={{ height: `${barHeightPercent}%` }}
                                                                                layoutId={`trend-bar-${barIdx}-${selectedPatient.id}`}
                                                                            />

                                                                            {/* Floating active pointer flags for SpO2 and Temp above bar */}
                                                                            <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 right-0 flex justify-center items-center gap-1 opacity-0 group-hover/trend:opacity-100 transition-opacity">
                                                                                <span className="text-[9px] font-mono font-bold text-emerald-600 bg-emerald-50 border border-emerald-100 px-1 rounded">
                                                                                    {dayRow.spo2}%
                                                                                </span>
                                                                                <span className="text-[9px] font-mono font-bold text-amber-600 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-100 px-1 rounded">
                                                                                    {dayRow.temperature}°C
                                                                                </span>
                                                                            </div>
                                                                        </div>

                                                                        {/* Calendar labels */}
                                                                        <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold font-mono tracking-tight mt-3 select-none ${isHovered ? 'text-[#B76CF6]' : 'text-slate-400'}`}>
                                                                            {dayRow.day.split(' ')[0]}
                                                                        </span>
                                                                        <span className="text-[8px] font-medium font-mono text-slate-400/80 mt-0.5 pointer-events-none">
                                                                            {dayRow.date.slice(5)}
                                                                        </span>
                                                                    </div>
                                                                );
                                                            })}
                                                        </div>

                                                        {/* X and Y labels for trends chart */}
                                                        <div className="pt-3 flex items-center justify-between text-[10px] font-mono text-slate-400 uppercase font-black tracking-wider select-none">
                                                            <span>Y: Heart Rate (BPM Scale Axis)</span>
                                                            <span>Scope: 6 Continuous Days Analysis</span>
                                                        </div>
                                                    </div>
                                                </div>
                                            )}

                                            {/* TAB 3: Threshold Settings & Clinical Event Simulator */}
                                            {activeDiagTab === 'thresholds' && (
                                                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-7">

                                                    {/* Range Sliders Controls for Adjustable Alarm Margins */}
                                                    <div className="bg-slate-50/80 border border-slate-100 p-5 rounded-3xl space-y-5">
                                                        <div>
                                                            <span className="text-[10px] font-mono uppercase text-[#B76CF6] font-bold tracking-widest leading-none">ALARM CONTROLS</span>
                                                            <h4 className="text-sm font-extrabold text-[#15151B] mt-1">Adjustable Safety Alarm Bounds</h4>
                                                            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
                                                                Modify physiological margins for {selectedPatient.name}. Exceeding active margins logs real-time dashboard warning banners.
                                                            </p>
                                                        </div>

                                                        <div className="space-y-4">
                                                            {/* Maximum Heart Rate range slide */}
                                                            <div>
                                                                <div className="flex justify-between text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                                                                    <span>Max Heart Rate Alert</span>
                                                                    <span className="font-mono bg-white border px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[#B76CF6]">
                                                                        {thresholds.maxHr} BPM
                                                                    </span>
                                                                </div>
                                                                <input
                                                                    type="range"
                                                                    min="90"
                                                                    max="170"
                                                                    value={thresholds.maxHr}
                                                                    onChange={(e) => {
                                                                        const val = parseInt(e.target.value);
                                                                        setCustomThresholds(prev => ({
                                                                            ...prev,
                                                                            [selectedPatient.id]: {
                                                                                ...prev[selectedPatient.id] || { minHr: 50, maxHr: 110, minSpo2: 93, maxTemp: 38.0 },
                                                                                maxHr: val
                                                                            }
                                                                        }));
                                                                    }}
                                                                    className="w-full accent-[#B76CF6]"
                                                                />
                                                            </div>

                                                            {/* Minimum Heart Rate range slide */}
                                                            <div>
                                                                <div className="flex justify-between text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">
                                                                    <span>Min Heart Rate Alert</span>
                                                                    <span className="font-mono bg-white border px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-indigo-500">
                                                                        {thresholds.minHr} BPM
                                                                    </span>
                                                                </div>
                                                                <input
                                                                    type="range"
                                                                    min="35"
                                                                    max="80"
                                                                    value={thresholds.minHr}
                                                                    onChange={(e) => {
